``\\\"\\\"``)\",\"* ``install_dir``: A string that states the directory into which all files are installed in the ``iso`` and ``netboot``\",\"  build modes (defaults to ``mkarchiso``). It must consist of **only** ``[a-z0-9]``. For the ``iso`` build mode, it must not\",\"  exceed 30 characters.\",\"* ``buildmodes``: An optional list of strings, that state the build modes that the profile uses. Only the following are\",\"  understood:\",\"\",\"  - ``bootstrap``: Build a compressed file containing a minimal system to bootstrap from\",\"  - ``iso``: Build a bootable ISO image (implicit default, if no ``buildmodes`` are set)\",\"  - ``netboot``: Build artifacts required for netboot using iPXE\",\"* ``bootmodes``: A list of strings, that state the supported boot modes of the resulting image. Only the following are\",\"  understood:\",\"\",\"  - ``bios.syslinux``: Syslinux for x86 BIOS booting\",\"  - ``uefi.grub``: GRUB for UEFI booting. For the x86_64 architecture, in addition to x64 UEFI, support for mixed-mode\",\"    booting (IA32 UEFI) will also be added.\",\"  - ``uefi.systemd-boot``: systemd-boot for UEFI booting. For the x86_64 architecture, in addition to x64 UEFI, support\",\"    for mixed-mode booting (IA32 UEFI) will also be added.\",\"* ``arch``: The architecture (e.g. ``x86_64``) to build the image for (defaults to the value returned by ``uname -m``).\",\"  This is also used to resolve the name of the packages file (e.g. ``packages.x86_64``)\",\"* ``packages``: File path to a text file containing a list of packages to install into the environment in ``iso`` and\",\"  ``netboot`` build modes (defaults to ``packages.${arch}``).\",\"* ``bootstrap_packages``: File path to a text file containing a list of packages to install into the environment in the\",\"  ``bootstrap`` build mode (defaults to ``bootstrap_packages.${arch}``).\",\"* ``pacman_conf``: The ``pacman.conf`` to use to install packages to the work directory when creating the image (defaults to\",\"  the host's ``/etc/pacman.conf``)\",\"* ``airootfs_image_type``: The image type to create. The following options are understood (defaults to ``squashfs``):\",\"\",\"  - ``squashfs``: Create a squashfs image directly from the airootfs work directory\",\"  - ``ext4+squashfs``: Create an ext4 partition, copy the airootfs work directory to it and create a squashfs image from it\",\"  - ``erofs``: Create an EROFS image for the airootfs work directory\",\"* ``airootfs_image_tool_options``: An array of options to pass to the tool to create the airootfs image. ``mksquashfs`` and\",\"  ``mkfs.erofs`` are supported. See ``mksquashfs --help`` or ``mkfs.erofs --help`` for all possible options\",\"* ``bootstrap_tarball_compression``: An array containing the compression program and arguments passed to it for\",\"  compressing the bootstrap tarball (defaults to ``cat``). For example: ``bootstrap_tarball_compression=(zstd -c -T0 --long -19)``.\",\"* ``file_permissions``: An associative array that lists files and/or directories who need specific ownership or\",\"  permissions. The array's keys contain the path and the value is a colon separated list of owner UID, owner GID and\",\"  access mode. E.g. ``file_permissions=([\\\"/etc/shadow\\\"]=\\\"0:0:400\\\")``. When directories are listed with a trailing backslash (``/``) **all** files and directories contained within the listed directory will have the same owner UID, owner GID, and access mode applied recursively.\",\"\",\"bootstrap_packages.arch\",\"=======================\",\"\",\"All packages to be installed into the environment of a bootstrap image have to be listed in a file specified by the\",\"``bootstrap_packages`` variable. If the variable is not specified, the architecture specific\",\"``bootstrap_packages.${arch}`` file (e.g. ``bootstrap_packages.x86_64``) or the ``bootstrap_packages`` file which reside\",\"top-level in the profile will be used instead.\",\"\",\"Packages have to be listed one per line. Lines starting with a ``#`` and blank lines are ignored.\",\"\",\"This file is required when generating bootstrap images using the ``bootstrap`` build mode.\",\"\",\"packages.arch\",\"=============\",\"\",\"All packages to be installed into the environment of an ISO image have to be listed in a file specified by the\",\"``packages`` variable. If the variable is not specified, the architecture specific ``packages.${arch}`` file (e.g.\",\"``packages.x86_64``) or the ``packages`` file which reside top-level in the profile will be used instead.\",\"\",\"Packages have to be listed one per line. Lines starting with a ``#`` and blank lines are ignored.\",\"\",\"  .. note::\",\"\",\"    The **mkinitcpio** and **mkinitcpio-archiso** packages are mandatory (see `#30\",\"    \\u003chttps://gitlab.archlinux.org/archlinux/archiso/-/issues/30\\u003e`_).\",\"\",\"This file is required when generating ISO images using the ``iso`` or ``netboot`` build modes.\",\"\",\"pacman.conf\",\"===========\",\"\",\"A configuration for pacman is required per profile.\",\"\",\"Some configuration options will not be used or will be modified:\",\"\",\"* ``CacheDir``: the profile's option is **only** used if it is not the default (i.e. ``/var/cache/pacman/pkg``) and if it is\",\"  not the same as the system's option. In all other cases the system's pacman cache is used.\",\"* ``HookDir``: it is **always** set to the ``/etc/pacman.d/hooks`` directory in the work directory's airootfs to allow\",\"  modification via the profile and ensure interoparability with hosts using dracut (see `#73\",\"  \\u003chttps://gitlab.archlinux.org/archlinux/archiso/-/issues/73\\u003e`_)\",\"* ``RootDir``: it is **always** removed, as setting it explicitely otherwise refers to the host's root filesystem (see\",\"  ``man 8 pacman`` for further information on the ``-r`` option used by ``pacstrap``)\",\"* ``LogFile``: it is **always** removed, as setting it explicitely otherwise refers to the host's pacman log file (see\",\"  ``man 8 pacman`` for further information on the ``-r`` option used by ``pacstrap``)\",\"* ``DBPath``: it is **always** removed, as setting it explicitely otherwise refers to the host's pacman database (see\",\"  ``man 8 pacman`` for further information on the ``-r`` option used by ``pacstrap``)\",\"\",\"airootfs\",\"========\",\"\",\"This optional directory may contain files and directories that will be copied to the work directory of the resulting\",\"image's root filesystem.\",\"The files are copied before packages are being installed to work directory location.\",\"Ownership and permissions of files and directories from the profile's ``airootfs`` directory are not preserved. The mode\",\"will be ``644`` for files and ``755`` for directories, all of them will be owned by root. To set custom ownership and/or\",\"permissions, use ``file_permissions`` in ``profiledef.sh``.\",\"\",\"With this overlay structure it is possible to e.g. create users and set passwords for them, by providing\",\"``airootfs/etc/passwd``, ``airootfs/etc/shadow``, ``airootfs/etc/gshadow`` (see ``man 5 passwd``, ``man 5 shadow`` and ``man 5 gshadow`` respectively).\",\"If user home directories exist in the profile's ``airootfs``, their ownership and (and top-level) permissions will be\",\"altered according to the provided information in the password file.\",\"\",\"Boot loader configuration\",\"=========================\",\"\",\"A profile may contain configuration for several boot loaders. These reside in specific top-level directories, which are\",\"explained in the following subsections.\",\"\",\"The following *custom template identifiers* are understood and will be replaced according to the assignments of the\",\"respective variables in ``profiledef.sh``:\",\"\",\"* ``%ARCHISO_LABEL%``: Set this using the ``iso_label`` variable in ``profiledef.sh``.\",\"* ``%INSTALL_DIR%``: Set this using the ``install_dir`` variable in ``profiledef.sh``.\",\"* ``%ARCH%``: Set this using the ``arch`` variable in ``profiledef.sh``.\",\"\",\"Additionally there are also *custom template identifiers* have harcoded values set by ``mkarchiso`` that cannot be\",\"overridden:\",\"\",\"* ``%ARCHISO_UUID%``: the ISO 9660 modification date in UTC, i.e. its \\\"UUID\\\",\",\"* ``%ARCHISO_SEARCH_FILENAME%``: file path on ISO 9660 that can be used by GRUB to find the ISO volume\",\"  (**for GRUB ``.cfg`` files only**).\",\"\",\"efiboot\",\"-------\",\"\",\"This directory is mandatory when the ``uefi.systemd-boot`` bootmode is selected in ``profiledef.sh``.\",\"It contains configuration for `systemd-boot\",\"\\u003chttps://www.freedesktop.org/wiki/Software/systemd/systemd-boot/\\u003e`_.\",\"\",\"  .. note::\",\"\",\"    The directory is a top-level representation of the systemd-boot configuration directories and files found in the\",\"    root of an EFI system partition.\",\"\",\"The *custom template identifiers* are **only** understood in the boot loader entry `.conf` files (i.e. **not** in\",\"``loader.conf``). Boot entries for foreign UEFI architectures will be skipped with the exception of IA32 boot entries\",\"when building for the x86_64 architecture.\",\"\",\"syslinux\",\"--------\",\"\",\"This directory is mandatory when the ``bios.syslinux`` bootmode is selected in ``profiledef.sh``.\",\"It contains configuration files for `syslinux \\u003chttps://wiki.syslinux.org/wiki/index.php?title=SYSLINUX\\u003e`_ or `isolinux\",\"\\u003chttps://wiki.syslinux.org/wiki/index.php?title=ISOLINUX\\u003e`_ , or `pxelinux\",\"\\u003chttps://wiki.syslinux.org/wiki/index.php?title=PXELINUX\\u003e`_ used in the resulting image.\",\"\",\"The *custom template identifiers* are understood in all `.cfg` files in this directory.\",\"\",\"grub\",\"----\",\"\",\"This directory is mandatory when the ``uefi.grub`` bootmode is selected in ``profiledef.sh``.\",\"It contains configuration files for `GRUB \\u003chttps://www.gnu.org/software/grub/\\u003e`_\",\"used in the resulting image.\"],\"stylingDirectives\":[[[0,7,\"pl-mh\"]],[],[[0,7,\"pl-mh\"]],[],[],[],[[3,7,\"pl-c1\"],[10,19,\"pl-en\"],[19,19,\"pl-c1\"]],[[0,0,\"pl-c1\"]],[[0,11,\"pl-c1\"]],[[0,16,\"pl-c1\"]],[[0,15,\"pl-c1\"]],[[0,16,\"pl-c1\"]],[[0,12,\"pl-c1\"]],[[0,30,\"pl-c1\"]],[[0,20,\"pl-c1\"]],[[0,18,\"pl-c1\"]],[[0,20,\"pl-c1\"]],[[0,0,\"pl-c1\"]],[[0,0,\"pl-c1\"]],[],[],[[0,13,\"pl-mh\"]],[],[],[],[],[[62,75,\"pl-c1\"],[81,116,\"pl-c1\"]],[[8,35,\"pl-c1\"]],[],[[4,12,\"pl-c1\"],[81,90,\"pl-c1\"]],[[4,13,\"pl-c1\"],[55,64,\"pl-c1\"]],[[4,17,\"pl-c1\"],[104,113,\"pl-c1\"]],[[4,19,\"pl-c1\"]],[[7,20,\"pl-c1\"]],[[4,15,\"pl-c1\"],[90,92,\"pl-c1\"]],[[4,15,\"pl-c1\"],[98,101,\"pl-c1\"],[110,117,\"pl-c1\"]],[[29,38,\"pl-c1\"],[64,68,\"pl-mb\"],[73,81,\"pl-c1\"],[95,98,\"pl-c1\"]],[],[[4,14,\"pl-c1\"]],[],[],[[6,15,\"pl-c1\"]],[[6,9,\"pl-c1\"],[67,77,\"pl-c1\"]],[[6,13,\"pl-c1\"]],[[4,13,\"pl-c1\"]],[],[],[[6,19,\"pl-c1\"]],[[6,15,\"pl-c1\"]],[],[[6,23,\"pl-c1\"]],[],[[4,8,\"pl-c1\"],[37,43,\"pl-c1\"],[107,115,\"pl-c1\"]],[[69,84,\"pl-c1\"]],[[4,12,\"pl-c1\"],[108,111,\"pl-c1\"]],[[4,11,\"pl-c1\"],[41,57,\"pl-c1\"]],[[4,22,\"pl-c1\"]],[[4,13,\"pl-c1\"],[42,68,\"pl-c1\"]],[[4,15,\"pl-c1\"],[25,36,\"pl-c1\"]],[[15,31,\"pl-c1\"]],[[4,23,\"pl-c1\"],[105,113,\"pl-c1\"]],[],[[6,14,\"pl-c1\"]],[[6,19,\"pl-c1\"]],[[6,11,\"pl-c1\"]],[[4,31,\"pl-c1\"],[107,117,\"pl-c1\"]],[[4,14,\"pl-c1\"],[38,55,\"pl-c1\"],[63,80,\"pl-c1\"]],[[4,33,\"pl-c1\"]],[[51,54,\"pl-c1\"],[74,128,\"pl-c1\"]],[[4,20,\"pl-c1\"]],[],[[22,66,\"pl-c1\"],[127,128,\"pl-c1\"],[134,137,\"pl-mb\"]],[],[],[[0,23,\"pl-mh\"]],[],[],[[2,20,\"pl-c1\"]],[[2,28,\"pl-c1\"],[44,69,\"pl-c1\"],[82,100,\"pl-c1\"]],[],[],[[65,66,\"pl-c1\"]],[],[[67,76,\"pl-c1\"]],[],[],[[0,13,\"pl-mh\"]],[],[],[[2,10,\"pl-c1\"],[85,101,\"pl-c1\"]],[[2,17,\"pl-c1\"],[30,38,\"pl-c1\"]],[],[[65,66,\"pl-c1\"]],[],[[5,9,\"pl-c1\"]],[],[[10,20,\"pl-mb\"],[29,47,\"pl-mb\"],[79,82,\"pl-corl\"]],[[4,64,\"pl-corl\"]],[],[[61,64,\"pl-c1\"],[72,79,\"pl-c1\"]],[],[], actions</span></div></div></div></div><div></div></div><div class=\"BlobViewContent-module__blobContentWrapper__JS0W6\"><section aria-labelledby=\"file-name-id-wide file-name-id-mobile\" class=\"BlobContent-module__blobContentSection__VOgZq BlobContent-module__blobContentSectionMarkdown__mPLOK\" style=\"margin-top:46px\"><div class=\"js-snippet-clipboard-copy-unpositioned BlobContent-module__markdownBlob__T8jpG\" data-hpc=\"true\" containertiming=\"hpc\"><article class=\"markdown-body entry-content container-lg\" itemprop=\"text\"><div class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"><h1 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" dir=\"auto\">profile</h1><a id=\"user-content-profile\" class=\"anchor\" aria-label=\"Permalink: profile\" href=\"#profile\"><svg data-component=\"Octicon\" class=\"octicon octicon-link\" viewBox=\"0 0 16 16\" version=\"1.1\" width=\"16\" height=\"16\" aria-hidden=\"true\"><path d=\"m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z\"></path></svg></a></div>\n<p dir=\"auto\">An archiso profile consists of several configuration files and a directory for files to be added to the resulting image.</p>\n<pre lang=\"plaintext\">profile/\n├── airootfs/\n├── efiboot/\n├── syslinux/\n├── grub/\n├── bootstrap_packages.arch\n├── packages.arch\n├── pacman.conf\n└── profiledef.sh\n</pre>\n<p dir=\"auto\">The required files and directories are explained in the following sections.</p>\n<a name=\"user-content-profiledef-sh\"></a>\n<div class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"><h2 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" dir=\"auto\">profiledef.sh</h2><a id=\"user-content-profiledefsh\" class=\"anchor\" aria-label=\"Permalink: profiledef.sh\" href=\"#profiledefsh\"><svg data-component=\"Octicon\" class=\"octicon octicon-link\" viewBox=\"0 0 16 16\" version=\"1.1\" width=\"16\" height=\"16\" aria-hidden=\"true\"><path d=\"m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z\"></path></svg></a></div>\n<p dir=\"auto\">This file describes several attributes of the resulting image and is a place for customization to the general behavior\nof the image.</p>\n<p dir=\"auto\">The image file is constructed from some of the variables in <code>profiledef.sh</code>: <code>&lt;iso_name&gt;-&lt;iso_version&gt;-&lt;arch&gt;.iso</code>\n(e.g. <code>archlinux-202010-x86_64.iso</code>).</p>\n<ul dir=\"auto\">\n<li><code>iso_name</code>: The first part of the name of the resulting image (defaults to <code>mkarchiso</code>)</li>\n<li><code>iso_label</code>: The ISO's volume label (defaults to <code>MKARCHISO</code>)</li>\n<li><code>iso_publisher</code>: A free-form string that states the publisher of the resulting image (defaults to <code>mkarchiso</code>)</li>\n<li><code>iso_application</code>: A free-form string that states the application (i.e. its use-case) of the resulting image (defaults\nto <code>mkarchiso iso</code>)</li>\n<li><code>iso_version</code>: A string that states the version of the resulting image (defaults to <code>\"\"</code>)</li>\n<li><code>install_dir</code>: A string that states the directory into which all files are installed in the <code>iso</code> and <code>netboot</code>\nbuild modes (defaults to <code>mkarchiso</code>). It must consist of <strong>only</strong> <code>[a-z0-9]</code>. For the <code>iso</code> build mode, it must not\nexceed 30 characters.</li>\n<li><code>buildmodes</code>: An optional list of strings, that state the build modes that the profile uses. Only the following are\nunderstood:<ul dir=\"auto\">\n<li><code>bootstrap</code>: Build a compressed file containing a minimal system to bootstrap from</li>\n<li><code>iso</code>: Build a bootable ISO image (implicit default, if no <code>buildmodes</code> are set)</li>\n<li><code>netboot</code>: Build artifacts required for netboot using iPXE</li>\n</ul>\n</li>\n<li><code>bootmodes</code>: A list of strings, that state the supported boot modes of the resulting image. Only the following are\nunderstood:<ul dir=\"auto\">\n<li><code>bios.syslinux</code>: Syslinux for x86 BIOS booting</li>\n<li><code>uefi.grub</code>: GRUB for UEFI booting. For the x86_64 architecture, in addition to x64 UEFI, support for mixed-mode\nbooting (IA32 UEFI) will also be added.</li>\n<li><code>uefi.systemd-boot</code>: systemd-boot for UEFI booting. For the x86_64 architecture, in addition to x64 UEFI, support\nfor mixed-mode booting (IA32 UEFI) will also be added.</li>\n</ul>\n</li>\n<li><code>arch</code>: The architecture (e.g. <code>x86_64</code>) to build the image for (defaults to the value returned by <code>uname -m</code>).\nThis is also used to resolve the name of the packages file (e.g. <code>packages.x86_64</code>)</li>\n<li><code>packages</code>: File path to a text file containing a list of packages to install into the environment in <code>iso</code> and\n<code>netboot</code> build modes (defaults to <code>packages.${arch}</code>).</li>\n<li><code>bootstrap_packages</code>: File path to a text file containing a list of packages to install into the environment in the\n<code>bootstrap</code> build mode (defaults to <code>bootstrap_packages.${arch}</code>).</li>\n<li><code>pacman_conf</code>: The <code>pacman.conf</code> to use to install packages to the work directory when creating the image (defaults to\nthe host's <code>/etc/pacman.conf</code>)</li>\n<li><code>airootfs_image_type</code>: The image type to create. The following options are understood (defaults to <code>squashfs</code>):<ul dir=\"auto\">\n<li><code>squashfs</code>: Create a squashfs image directly from the airootfs work directory</li>\n<li><code>ext4+squashfs</code>: Create an ext4 partition, copy the airootfs work directory to it and create a squashfs image from it</li>\n<li><code>erofs</code>: Create an EROFS image for the airootfs work directory</li>\n</ul>\n</li>\n<li><code>airootfs_image_tool_options</code>: An array of options to pass to the tool to create the airootfs image. <code>mksquashfs</code> and\n<code>mkfs.erofs</code> are supported. See <code>mksquashfs --help</code> or <code>mkfs.erofs --help</code> for all possible options</li>\n<li><code>bootstrap_tarball_compression</code>: An array containing the compression program and arguments passed to it for\ncompressing the bootstrap tarball (defaults to <code>cat</code>). For example: <code>bootstrap_tarball_compression=(zstd -c -T0 --long -19)</code>.</li>\n<li><code>file_permissions</code>: An associative array that lists files and/or directories who need specific ownership or\npermissions. The array's keys contain the path and the value is a colon separated list of owner UID, owner GID and\naccess mode. E.g. <code>file_permissions=([\"/etc/shadow\"]=\"0:0:400\")</code>. When directories are listed with a trailing backslash (<code>/</code>) <strong>all</strong> files and directories contained within the listed directory will have the same owner UID, owner GID, and access mode applied recursively.</li>\n</ul>\n<a name=\"user-content-bootstrap-packages-arch\"></a>\n<div class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"><h2 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" dir=\"auto\">bootstrap_packages.arch</h2><a id=\"user-content-bootstrap_packagesarch\" class=\"anchor\" aria-label=\"Permalink: bootstrap_packages.arch\" href=\"#bootstrap_packagesarch\"><svg data-component=\"Octicon\" class=\"octicon octicon-link\" viewBox=\"0 0 16 16\" version=\"1.1\" width=\"16\" height=\"16\" aria-hidden=\"true\"><path d=\"m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z\"></path></svg></a></div>\n<p dir=\"auto\">All packages to be installed into the environment of a bootstrap image have to be listed in a file specified by the\n<code>bootstrap_packages</code> variable. If the variable is not specified, the architecture specific\n<code>bootstrap_packages.${arch}</code> file (e.g. <code>bootstrap_packages.x86_64</code>) or the <code>bootstrap_packages</code> file which reside\ntop-level in the profile will be used instead.</p>\n<p dir=\"auto\">Packages have to be listed one per line. Lines starting with a <code>#</code> and blank lines are ignored.</p>\n<p dir=\"auto\">This file is required when generating bootstrap images using the <code>bootstrap</code> build mode.</p>\n<a name=\"user-content-packages-arch\"></a>\n<div class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"><h2 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" dir=\"auto\">packages.arch</h2><a id=\"user-content-packagesarch\" class=\"anchor\" aria-label=\"Permalink: packages.arch\" href=\"#packagesarch\"><svg data-component=\"Octicon\" class=\"octicon octicon-link\" viewBox=\"0 0 16 16\" version=\"1.1\" width=\"16\" height=\"16\" aria-hidden=\"true\"><path d=\"m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z\"></path></svg></a></div>\n<p dir=\"auto\">All packages to be installed into the environment of an ISO image have to be listed in a file specified by the\n<code>packages</code> variable. If the variable is not specified, the architecture specific <code>packages.${arch}</code> file (e.g.\n<code>packages.x86_64</code>) or the <code>packages</code> file which reside top-level in the profile will be used instead.</p>\n<p dir=\"auto\">Packages have to be listed one per line. Lines starting with a <code>#</code> and blank lines are ignored.</p>\n<blockquote>\n<div dir=\"auto\">\n<p dir=\"auto\">Note</p>\n<p dir=\"auto\">The <strong>mkinitcpio</strong> and <strong>mkinitcpio-archiso</strong> packages are mandatory (see <a href=\"https://gitlab.archlinux.org/archlinux/archiso/-/issues/30\" rel=\"nofollow\">#30</a>).</p>\n</div>\n</blockquote>\n<p dir=\"auto\">This file is required when generating ISO images using the <code>iso</code> or <code>netboot</code> build modes.</p>\n<a name=\"user-content-pacman-conf\"></a>\n<div class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"><h2 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" dir=\"auto\">pacman.conf</h2><a id=\"user-content-pacmanconf\" class=\"anchor\" aria-label=\"Permalink: pacman.conf\" href=\"#pacmanconf\"><svg data-component=\"Octicon\" class=\"octicon octicon-link\" viewBox=\"0 0 16 16\" version=\"1.1\" width=\"16\" height=\"16\" aria-hidden=\"true\"><path d=\"m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z\"></path></svg></a></div>\n<p dir=\"auto\">A configuration for pacman is required per profile.</p>\n<p dir=\"auto\">Some configuration options will not be used or will be modified:</p>\n<ul dir=\"auto\">\n<li><code>CacheDir</code>: the profile's option is <strong>only</strong> used if it is not the default (i.e. <code>/var/cache/pacman/pkg</code>) and if it is\nnot the same as the system's option. In all other cases the system's pacman cache is used.</li>\n<li><code>HookDir</code>: it is <strong>always</strong> set to the <code>/etc/pacman.d/hooks</code> directory in the work directory's airootfs to allow\nmodification via the profile and ensure interoparability with hosts using dracut (see <a href=\"https://gitlab.archlinux.org/archlinux/archiso/-/issues/73\" rel=\"nofollow\">#73</a>)</li>\n<li><code>RootDir</code>: it is <strong>always</strong> removed, as setting it explicitely otherwise refers to the host's root filesystem (see\n<code>man 8 pacman</code> for further information on the <code>-r</code> option used by <code>pacstrap</code>)</li>\n<li><code>LogFile</code>: it is <strong>always</strong> removed, as setting it explicitely otherwise refers to the host's pacman log file (see\n<code>man 8 pacman</code> for further information on the <code>-r</code> option used by <code>pacstrap</code>)</li>\n<li><code>DBPath</code>: it is <strong>always</strong> removed, as setting it explicitely otherwise refers to the host's pacman database (see\n<code>man 8 pacman</code> for further information on the <code>-r</code> option used by <code>pacstrap</code>)</li>\n</ul>\n<a name=\"user-content-airootfs\"></a>\n<div class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"><h2 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" dir=\"auto\">airootfs</h2><a id=\"user-content-airootfs\" class=\"anchor\" aria-label=\"Permalink: airootfs\" href=\"#airootfs\"><svg data-component=\"Octicon\" class=\"octicon octicon-link\" viewBox=\"0 0 16 16\" version=\"1.1\" width=\"16\" height=\"16\" aria-hidden=\"true\"><path d=\"m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z\"></path></svg></a></div>\n<p dir=\"auto\">This optional directory may contain files and directories that will be copied to the work directory of the resulting\nimage's root filesystem.\nThe files are copied before packages are being installed to work directory location.\nOwnership and permissions of files and directories from the profile's <code>airootfs</code> directory are not preserved. The mode\nwill be <code>644</code> for files and <code>755</code> for directories, all of them will be owned by root. To set custom ownership and/or\npermissions, use <code>file_permissions</code> in <code>profiledef.sh</code>.</p>\n<p dir=\"auto\">With this overlay structure it is possible to e.g. create users and set passwords for them, by providing\n<code>airootfs/etc/passwd</code>, <code>airootfs/etc/shadow</code>, <code>airootfs/etc/gshadow</code> (see <code>man 5 passwd</code>, <code>man 5 shadow</code> and <code>man 5 gshadow</code> respectively).\nIf user home directories exist in the profile's <code>airootfs</code>, their ownership and (and top-level) permissions will be\naltered according to the provided information in the password file.</p>\n<a name=\"user-content-boot-loader-configuration\"></a>\n<div class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"><h2 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" dir=\"auto\">Boot loader configuration</h2><a id=\"user-content-boot-loader-configuration\" class=\"anchor\" aria-label=\"Permalink: Boot loader configuration\" href=\"#boot-loader-configuration\"><svg data-component=\"Octicon\" class=\"octicon octicon-link\" viewBox=\"0 0 16 16\" version=\"1.1\" width=\"16\" height=\"16\" aria-hidden=\"true\"><path d=\"m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z\"></path></svg></a></div>\n<p dir=\"auto\">A profile may contain configuration for several boot loaders. These reside in specific top-level directories, which are\nexplained in the following subsections.</p>\n<p dir=\"auto\">The following <em>custom template identifiers</em> are understood and will be replaced according to the assignments of the\nrespective variables in <code>profiledef.sh</code>:</p>\n<ul dir=\"auto\">\n<li><code>%ARCHISO_LABEL%</code>: Set this using the <code>iso_label</code> variable in <code>profiledef.sh</code>.</li>\n<li><code>%INSTALL_DIR%</code>: Set this using the <code>install_dir</code> variable in <code>profiledef.sh</code>.</li>\n<li><code>%ARCH%</code>: Set this using the <code>arch</code> variable in <code>profiledef.sh</code>.</li>\n</ul>\n<p dir=\"auto\">Additionally there are also <em>custom template identifiers</em> have harcoded values set by <code>mkarchiso</code> that cannot be\noverridden:</p>\n<ul dir=\"auto\">\n<li><code>%ARCHISO_UUID%</code>: the ISO 9660 modification date in UTC, i.e. its \"UUID\",</li>\n<li><code>%ARCHISO_SEARCH_FILENAME%</code>: file path on ISO 9660 that can be used by GRUB to find the ISO volume\n(<strong>for GRUB ``.cfg`` files only</strong>).</li>\n</ul>\n<a name=\"user-content-efiboot\"></a>\n<div class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"><h3 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" dir=\"auto\">efiboot</h3><a id=\"user-content-efiboot\" class=\"anchor\" aria-label=\"Permalink: efiboot\" href=\"#efiboot\"><svg data-component=\"Octicon\" class=\"octicon octicon-link\" viewBox=\"0 0 16 16\" version=\"1.1\" width=\"16\" height=\"16\" aria-hidden=\"true\"><path d=\"m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z\"></path></svg></a></div>\n<p dir=\"auto\">This directory is mandatory when the <code>uefi.systemd-boot</code> bootmode is selected in <code>profiledef.sh</code>.\nIt contains configuration for <a href=\"https://www.freedesktop.org/wiki/Software/systemd/systemd-boot/\" rel=\"nofollow\">systemd-boot</a>.</p>\n<blockquote>\n<div dir=\"auto\">\n<p dir=\"auto\">Note</p>\n<p dir=\"auto\">The directory is a top-level representation of the systemd-boot configuration directories and files found in the\nroot of an EFI system partition.</p>\n</div>\n</blockquote>\n<p dir=\"auto\">The <em>custom template identifiers</em> are <strong>only</strong> understood in the boot loader entry .conf files (i.e. <strong>not</strong> in\n<code>loader.conf</code>). Boot entries for foreign UEFI architectures will be skipped with the exception of IA32 boot entries\nwhen building for the x86_64 architecture.</p>\n<a name=\"user-content-syslinux\"></a>\n<div class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"><h3 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" dir=\"auto\">syslinux</h3><a id=\"user-content-syslinux\" class=\"anchor\" aria-label=\"Permalink: syslinux\" href=\"#syslinux\"><svg data-component=\"Octicon\" class=\"octicon octicon-link\" viewBox=\"0 0 16 16\" version=\"1.1\" width=\"16\" height=\"16\" aria-hidden=\"true\"><path d=\"m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z\"></path></svg></a></div>\n<p dir=\"auto\">This directory is mandatory when the <code>bios.syslinux</code> bootmode is selected in <code>profiledef.sh</code>.\nIt contains configuration files for <a href=\"https://wiki.syslinux.org/wiki/index.php?title=SYSLINUX\" rel=\"nofollow\">syslinux</a> or <a href=\"https://wiki.syslinux.org/wiki/index.php?title=ISOLINUX\" rel=\"nofollow\">isolinux</a> , or <a href=\"https://wiki.syslinux.org/wiki/index.php?title=PXELINUX\" rel=\"nofollow\">pxelinux</a> used in the resulting image.</p>\n<p dir=\"auto\">The <em>custom template identifiers</em> are understood in all .cfg files in this directory.</p>\n<a name=\"user-content-grub\"></a>\n<div class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"><h3 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" dir=\"auto\">grub</h3><a id=\"user-content-grub\" class=\"anchor\" aria-label=\"Permalink: grub\" href=\"#grub\"><svg data-component=\"Octicon\" class=\"octicon octicon-link\" viewBox=\"0 0 16 16\" version=\"1.1\" width=\"16\" height=\"16\" aria-hidden=\"true\"><path d=\"m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z\"></path></svg></a></div>\n<p dir=\"auto\">This directory is mandatory when the <code>uefi.grub</code> bootmode is selected in <code>profiledef.sh</code>.\nIt contains configuration files for <a href=\"https://www.gnu.org/software/grub/\" rel=\"nofollow\">GRUB</a>\nused in the resulting image.</p>\n\n</article></div><div class=\"d-none\"></div></section></div></div></div> </div> <!-- -->
